### 2026-10-14 14:54:52  #其他

【助力“中國服務”出海 第十屆服務貿易標準化會議在京舉辦】今天（10月14日）上午，由中國貿促會主辦的第十屆服務貿易標準化會議在北京舉行。來自國際組織、政府部門、行業機構和企業的代表，圍繞服務貿易國際規則銜接、標準應用和跨境經營展開交流，共同推動標準合作。會上集中釋出了三項國際標準成果，分別涉及可持續旅遊指標的使用、電子商務平臺使用者條款編制，以及共享製造的概念與模式。同時啟動了十三項國際標準研製專案，覆蓋可持續旅遊、電子商務、共享製造等領域，推動標準與產業需求對接，助力“中國服務”拓展國際市場。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-14 14:54:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Stock Market #Importance 1

Nikkei Extends Losing Streak to Five Sessions — Japan’s Nikkei 225 slipped 0.05% to close at 67,744 on Wednesday, surrendering earlier gains and extending its decline to a fifth consecutive session. Sentiment weakened after Dutch chip-equipment maker ASML said it would be unable to fully meet demand over the next two years, fueling concerns over the sustainability of the AI investment boom. Investors also remained cautious as rising oil prices and persistent supply disruptions in the Middle East heightened inflationary risks. Earlier in the session, equities drew some support from dovish remarks by New York Fed President John Williams, who said one additional rate hike may be appropriate this year but saw no need for further tightening in 2027. Technology and banking stocks led the declines, with Kioxia Holdings falling 3.2%, Lasertec dropping 1.6%, Tokyo Electron losing 2.7%, Mitsubishi UFJ declining 1.9%, and Mizuho Financial sliding 2.9%.

### 2026-10-14 14:49:15  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【光大期貨：CPI前夜黃金震盪，市場屏息以待】10月14日，倫敦現貨黃金隔夜小幅回落，COMEX黃金期貨跌0.15%。宏觀方面，美聯儲三把手威廉姆斯稱，今年可能再加息一次，但2027年沒有必要繼續加息；美聯儲理事沃勒稱美長期國債收益率上升正在抑制房地產、汽車等利率敏感領域的需求，在一定程度上發揮了收緊貨幣政策的作用。今晚美CPI資料公佈，影響後市加息預期，當前市場預計美聯儲10月28日會議上加息機率較低，但核心CPI環比若升至0.3%可能重新點燃加息擔憂。重磅資料公佈前，資金謹慎，短線黃金維持低位震盪。IMF最新《世界經濟展望》顯示，發達經濟體今年淨借款規模將達GDP的5.2%，創2021年以來新高；美國一般政府淨借款將達GDP的8.5%，高於2025年的7.1%，較今年4月的判斷進一步惡化。地緣政治方面，特朗普13日表示已與沙特王儲通話，但拒絕透露是否已決定加入打擊胡塞武裝的行動，並指胡塞武裝在美國中期選舉前蓄意推高油價。黃金價格仍在低位反覆波動，這可能與美長債收益率始終維持在偏高位有關，對市場而言存在兩個矛盾，一方面擔憂美長債收益率失控風險，但另一方面也提振了美元資產的吸引力。綜合來看，美經濟資料走弱預期抑制了貴金屬的短期下行空間，但長端利率高企或加息預期與美元強勢仍構成估值約束，仍宜謹慎看待。（光大期貨）

### 2026-10-14 14:48:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Slides to 4-Year Low — Iron ore futures fell toward CNY 670 per ton, touching fresh four-year lows as increased blast furnace maintenance in China and abundant global supply weighed on prices. Industry data showed that projected hot metal production losses from blast furnace maintenance increased for a third consecutive week during October 10–16, with cumulative losses rising by more than 270,000 tons since late September. Meanwhile, iron ore inventories at seven major ports in Australia and Brazil climbed by 1.99 million tons to 12.58 million tons in the week ended October 11. Global mining capacity is also expanding, while producers are increasing fourth-quarter shipments to meet quarterly and annual targets. Further dampening sentiment, the World Steel Association forecast a 0.1% contraction in global steel demand in 2026, reversing its April projection of 0.3% growth amid weakening demand in China and disruptions linked to the US-Iran conflict.

### 2026-10-14 14:46:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 2

India Wholesale Price Inflation Tops Forecasts — India’s wholesale prices increased 11.39% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from a 9.92% rise in August and surpassing expectations of 10.18%. The data have been based on the new 2022–23 base year since September 2025. The acceleration was driven by faster increases in fuel, manufacturing, and food prices. Fuel inflation accelerated to 25.85% from 22.93% in August, mainly driven by faster increases in mineral oil prices (40.02% vs 38.48% in August) and crude petroleum and natural gas prices (57.32% vs 34.41%). Meanwhile, food inflation accelerated to 8.66% from 7.05% in August, as food article prices rose faster (8.14% vs 5.67%), while non-food article prices remained elevated but increased at a slower pace (10.28% vs 14.79%). Manufacturing inflation also accelerated to 9.76% from 8.37%, led by chemicals and chemical products (16.74%), tobacco products (12.90%), textiles (13.85%), rubber and plastics (13.47%), and basic metals (16.96%).

### 2026-10-14 14:40:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Household Spending MoM #Importance 1

Sweden Household Spending Rebounds in September — Household spending in Sweden rose 1.1% month-on-month in September 2026, rebounding from a revised 0.4% drop in the previous month. Spending accelerated on furnishings, household equipment, and routine home maintenance (2.7% vs 1.0% in August), transportation (1.8% vs 1.2%), information and communication (2.4% vs 0.2%), personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (3.6% vs 0.1%), and healthcare (0.3% vs 0.0%). At the same time, consumption recovered in food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.3% vs -1.3%), alcoholic beverages, tobacco, and drugs (3.6% vs -4.0%), clothing and footwear (3.1% vs -1.5%), and recreation, sports, and culture (3.2% vs -1.7%). Meanwhile, spending declined in housing and utilities (-0.4% vs 0.3%) and restaurants and accommodation (-0.3% vs 0.9%). On a yearly basis, household consumption climbed 3.5% in September 2026 from 2.3% in August.

UK Natural Gas Prices Hover Near 2022 High — UK natural gas prices fell to 213 pence per therm on Wednesday, but remained near their highest levels since 2022, as Middle Eastern LNG exports remained constrained and gas storage levels were still low. Although countries including the US, Canada, and Nigeria have increased LNG exports, curtailed supplies from the Persian Gulf has intensified competition for spot cargoes, limiting availability for Europe and making it harder for the region to rebuild inventories. Europe’s gas storage facilities are 73.94% full, still below historical averages. Forecasts of colder-than-usual weather could drive up heating demand and prompt earlier withdrawals from storage. Meanwhile, growing renewable output in the UK has reduced dependence on gas-fired electricity, but the country remains exposed to fuel price swings.

【芝商所拉塞爾·貝蒂：大商所向境外交易者開放大豆產品系列具有里程碑意義】芝加哥商業交易所集團董事總經理兼亞太區負責人拉塞爾·貝蒂在第19屆國際油脂油料大會暨農畜產業（衍生品）大會上表示，自2003年芝商所與大商所首次簽署諒解備忘錄以來，雙方建立了持久的合作伙伴關係。他指出，雙方市場互補，共同為市場參與者提供了大豆及其壓榨產品全球價格及本地價格的完整圖景。在兩個市場均深度充足、流動性強且值得信賴的基礎上，透過CBOT大豆期貨以及大商所豆粕和豆油期貨對壓榨利潤進行管理，已經成為行業內廣泛使用的對沖策略。拉塞爾·貝蒂表示，芝商所樂於見到大商所向境外交易者開放其大豆產品系列，這一舉措對大商所整個大豆類產品市場意味著更多參與者、更強流動性、更高價格完整性，惠及所有交易者並加強了國內價格與全球價格之間的聯絡，具有里程碑意義。中國是全球最大的油籽進口國和全球第二大經濟體，正以驚人的速度開放、深化並與世界連線，在此背景下芝商所重視中國市場並希望繼續加強與大商所的合作，分享市場結構方面專業知識，探索攜手服務市場參與者的路徑方法。（新華財經）

### 2026-10-14 14:25:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Swedish Inflation Hits 19-Month High — Sweden’s annual inflation rate rose to 1.1% in September 2026, slightly below the initial estimate of 1.2% but sharply up from 0.3% in August, marking its highest level since February 2025. The acceleration was driven mainly by higher energy prices, with fuel and electricity costs increasing by 20% and 17%, respectively. This pushed inflation in housing and utilities to 4.8% from 2.6% in August, partly reflecting higher rents and maintenance costs. Price growth also accelerated for transport (3.4% vs 1.7%) and information and communication (2.2% vs 1.8%). In contrast, food and non-alcoholic beverage prices continued to fall by 6.6%, while prices also declined for health (-5.9% vs -6.2%) and education services (-15.6% vs -15.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.9%, matching estimates and rebounding from a 0.3% decline in August. Meanwhile, the CPIF, which excludes the effect of changes in mortgage interest rates, rose to 1.5% from 0.7% in August.

【美國政府開始審理SpaceX星艦擴張提案：每年發射456次、著陸912次】美國聯邦監管機構週二宣佈，正在審查SpaceX的一項提案，以大幅擴充套件其位於得克薩斯州南部星際基地設施的運營，該計劃最終將使SpaceX每年能夠進行數百次發射和著陸。美國聯邦航空管理局表示，將就SpaceX擴建計劃編制環境影響報告書並舉行公眾聽證會。根據該提案，SpaceX的目標是最終在該設施每年進行多達456次發射和912次著陸。SpaceX的擴建計劃將分兩個階段進行。在第一階段，SpaceX每年將執行最多100次星艦發射和200次著陸，不限時間與地點，隨著飛行器可靠性的提高，SpaceX計劃在幾年內實施第二階段，最終達到每年456次發射的上限，且僅限工作日進行。912次著陸限制則反映了SpaceX得克薩斯州發射場允許的最大回收次數。著陸是指星艦飛船及其超重型助推器以直立、完整的狀態返回，以便快速重複使用。SpaceX也保留將火箭部件濺落在墨西哥灣或公海的選項。

### 2026-10-14 14:14:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 2

Germany Wholesale Inflation Hits More Than 3-Year High — Germany’s wholesale prices jumped by 8.7% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from 6.8% in the previous month. The latest figure marked the fastest price growth since February 2023, driven largely by sharp increases in energy and intermediate goods prices amid ongoing hostilities in the Middle East. Prices rose at a faster pace for mineral oil products (+48.8%), non-ferrous ores, metals and semi-finished metal products (+24.9%), and grain, raw tobacco, seeds and animal feed (+19.3%). Moreover, prices increased for chemical products (+16.4%) and data processing and peripheral equipment (+12.1%). In contrast, prices fell for live animals (-15.1%), milk, dairy products, eggs, edible fats and oils (-9.7%), and meat and meat products (-6.0%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices rose by 1.9% in September, accelerating from a 0.9% increase in the previous month. This marked the strongest monthly increase in five months and exceeded market expectations of 0.7%.

【多名美參議員致信特朗普要求撤銷美俄“柴油協議”】美國國會兩黨部分參議員13日致信美國總統特朗普，要求撤銷日前與俄羅斯就柴油貿易達成的協議。 在這封公開信中，聯合簽名的6名共和黨參議員和6名民主黨參議員稱，允許俄羅斯柴油進入全球市場對燃料價格的影響微乎其微，而俄羅斯由此獲得的收入將“無疑為其意圖進行的消耗戰提供資金”。 信中寫道：“允許莫斯科在全球市場上自由銷售俄羅斯柴油，不僅損害了美國的安全利益，也背叛了烏克蘭人民……這項協議是對我們對手的饋贈，也是對我們盟友的背叛，應當將其撤銷。” 這些參議員還要求特朗普利用9月簽署成法的《2026年格雷厄姆制裁俄羅斯和伊朗法案》，加大對俄羅斯等國的經濟施壓。 媒體報道指出，簽署這封公開信的包括前參議院共和黨領袖米奇·麥康奈爾及其他多名資深參議員。這說明特朗普所在的共和黨內的資深成員在11月3日中期選舉前與特朗普產生了分歧。 美國財政部外國資產控制辦公室9日釋出公告說，在未來幾個月放鬆針對俄羅斯柴油貿易的制裁，以緩解美國內柴油供應緊張、平抑油價。同日，特朗普宣佈，美俄領導人就俄向美國及全球市場提供柴油達成一致。(新華社)

### 2026-10-14 13:41:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Finnish Inflation Rate Highest Since 2024 — Finland’s annual inflation rate rose to 2.5% in September 2026 from 2.2% in the previous month, marking the highest reading since February 2024. Price growth particularly accelerated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.9% vs 1.6% in August), housing and utilities (1.6% vs 1.3%), and transport (8.9% vs 7.3%). Costs also rose at a faster pace for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.8% vs 3.6%), information and communication (4.5% vs 3.1%), restaurants and accommodation services (2.0% vs 1.4%), and insurance and financial services (1.7% vs 0.4%), while deflation eased for clothing and footwear (-0.9% vs -1.4%). On the other hand, prices slowed for furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (2.9% vs 4.7%), health (2.5% vs 3.0%), recreation and culture (1.5% vs 1.9%), and education (4.2% vs 5.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rebounded by 0.7% after falling 0.2% in the previous month.

ASX Dips 0.6% at Finish — The ASX 200 lost 56 points, or 0.6%, to close at 8,728 on Wednesday, reversing recent gains as financials, consumer durables, and transport weighed. Traders turned cautious ahead of the upcoming labor data, after August unemployment hit 4.6%, the highest since late 2021. Meanwhile, Australia’s leading index slipped 0.1% in September, its first decline since May, due to higher fuel costs and rising borrowing rates. Still, losses were partly offset by firmer U.S.stock futures ahead of a key inflation report amid elevated oil prices. In top trading partner China, September CPI was slightly below forecasts, but producer prices rose more than expected. Meanwhile, Chinese exports and imports hit record highs, driving a solid trade surplus. The big four banks fell between 0.9% and 2.3%, while BHP (-0.9%) and Rio Tinto (-0.3%) also lagged. Other laggards included Genesis Minerals (-3.9%), Evolution Mining (-2.9%), and Qantas Airways (-2.2%).

South Korean Won Rises Near 2-Year High — The South Korean won traded around 1,337 per dollar, approaching a recent two-year high, supported by a weaker dollar and continued exporter dollar sales. Markets are assigning an 88% probability to a rate hike in December. Strong semiconductor exports also continued to underpin foreign currency inflows, as chip exports exceeded USD 60 billion for the first time in September and local chipmakers converted overseas earnings into won. Meanwhile, elevated oil prices remained a risk, as higher energy import costs could widen Korea’s trade deficit and increase demand for dollars. Palm Oil Holds Above MYR 4,700 Despite Record Stocks — Malaysian palm oil futures stayed firm above MYR 4,700 per tonne, extending their upside momentum as strength in China’s Dalian edible oils and higher crude prices boosted palm’s competitiveness in biodiesel. Export signals were also upbeat, with cargo surveyors estimating October 1–10 exports jumped 33.5%–37.7% from the same period in September, while festive buying from top buyer India and firmer trading activity in China during September added support. Yet sentiment was checked by bearish monthly data from the Malaysian Palm Oil Board. September inventories surged 24.5% mom to a record 3.51million tonnes, the sixth monthly rise in a row. Output climbed 16.5% to 2.12million tonnes, the highest since August 2015, while exports fell 12.1% to 1.12 million tonnes, marking a second month of decline. Record-high stocks, stronger output, and weaker shipments underscored supply pressure, hinting that despite firmer near-term demand, further price gains may be capped by fundamentals.

Copper Extends Gains on Mounting Supply Disruptions — Copper futures rose above $6.70 per pound on Wednesday, moving closer to record highs as labor strikes and adverse weather conditions disrupted mining operations across Chile, the world’s largest copper producer. Workers at BHP Group’s massive Escondida mine are set to begin a strike after union leaders reported that wage negotiations failed to reach an agreement before the midnight deadline. Meanwhile, two unions at Antofagasta Plc’s Centinela mine have also launched strikes. Separately, two workers died on Sunday after an avalanche struck Codelco’s major El Teniente mine, where open-pit operations had already been suspended due to unfavorable weather. On the demand side, rising orders for data centers, semiconductor production, and battery storage systems supporting AI technologies and electric vehicles across Asia continued to bolster the outlook for copper.

### 2026-10-14 11:56:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

EU Natural Gas Prices Remain Near Multi-Year Highs — European natural gas prices fell below €86 per megawatt-hour on Wednesday but remained near their highest levels since 2022, as Middle Eastern LNG exports remained constrained and gas storage levels were still low. Although countries including the US, Canada, and Nigeria have increased LNG exports, curtailed supplies from the Persian Gulf has intensified competition for spot cargoes, limiting availability for Europe and making it harder for the region to rebuild inventories. Europe’s gas storage facilities are 73.94% full, still below historical averages. Forecasts of colder-than-usual weather, particularly in Central Europe, could drive up heating demand and prompt earlier withdrawals from storage. Meanwhile, unplanned outages at Norwegian gas infrastructure have also reduced pipeline export capacity to Europe. With low gas storage levels and uncertain Gulf supplies, Europe faces the risk of sharp price increases if a severe cold snap hits this winter.

Japan 10Y Yield Steadies on Cautious BOJ Outlook — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield steadied around 3% on Wednesday, holding a recent decline after reports suggested the Bank of Japan was reluctant to deliver a rare back-to-back interest rate hike this month, while leaving the door open for a potential increase in December. The central bank reportedly sees limited justification for consecutive rate hikes unless there are signs of a sharp acceleration in upside inflation risks. Meanwhile, BOJ Governor Kazuo Ueda said policymakers would continue to closely monitor bond yields, reiterating that long-term interest rates are determined by market forces. His comments signaled a cautious approach as the central bank moves further away from strict yield control. Elsewhere, oil prices extended gains amid escalating hostilities between Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi rebels, heightening concerns over potential supply disruptions in the Middle East and keeping inflationary pressures elevated.

【本屆APEC財長會將重點討論哪些議題？財政部有關負責人回應】據財政部訊息，第33屆亞太經濟合作組織（APEC）財長會將於10月20日至21日在中國香港舉行。來自21個APEC經濟體的財政部長或其代表、有關國際組織負責人和夥伴方高階代表將參加會議。中國財政部部長藍佛安將率中國代表團出席並主持會議，副部長廖岷代表中方發言。日前，財政部有關負責人就本屆財長會的背景及意義、主要議題、預期成果等問題接受記者採訪。本屆APEC財長會將重點討論哪些議題？中方對本屆APEC財長會成果有何期待？財政部有關負責人表示，圍繞APEC“中國年”主題和三大優先領域，結合當前亞太經濟發展面臨的新形勢，我們提出了財金渠道四項重點議題：支援民生髮展的財政政策、數字基礎設施融資、因地制宜促進普惠金融發展、促進APEC青年財經人才交流合作。這些議題既立足當前，也著眼長遠。目前，各經濟體普遍面臨穩增長、防風險、保民生的任務，亟需加強宏觀經濟政策溝通協調，採取適當財政金融政策更好支援創新、包容性發展；同時，數字化、人工智慧等新技術加快發展，既帶來了新的數字基礎設施投融資需求，也凸顯出加強青年財經人才交流互鑑、提升未來政策制定能力的重要性。今年以來，APEC財金渠道先後舉行APEC財政和央行副手會、APEC財政高官會以及普惠金融、數字基礎設施等專題研討會。各方圍繞全球和區域宏觀經濟金融形勢以及相關重點議題進行了深入交流，並形成系列共識，為財長會奠定了良好基礎。中方今年新發起的APEC青年財經人才交流合作專案已於8月下旬由亞太財經與發展學院實施，來自18個APEC經濟體的30餘名青年財經人才參加了該專案，大家圍繞“數字創新促進亞太經濟發展與增長”主題，分別在上海和廣西開展了現場考察、專題研討與經驗分享等，取得圓滿成功，並得到參與者的高度評價。在即將舉行的財長會上，各經濟體代表將進一步深入交換意見，圍繞重點議題形成共識檔案和相關成果，包括財長會宣告、亞太地區支援民生髮展的財政政策報告、數字基礎設施融資政策宣告、普惠金融創新實踐案例集以及繼續開展青年財經人才交流合作等。我們希望透過這些成果，總結各方有益經驗，推動政策交流和經驗分享，為今後深化亞太財金合作、支援亞太經濟增長提供支撐。

### 2026-10-14 11:31:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Currency #Importance 1

Offshore Yuan Trades Near Multi-Year High — The offshore yuan traded around 6.69 per dollar on Wednesday, trading near its storngest level since July 2022 as strong external demand continued to support China’s economic outlook. China’s trade surplus widened to $133.53 billion in September 2026 from $88.72 billion a year earlier, marking its largest surplus since January 2025. Exports surged 36.3% year-on-year to a record $447.18 billion while imports climbed 31.0% to a record $313.66 billion. China’s trade surplus totaled to $938 billion in the first nine months of the year, putting it on track to reach $1 trillion. Meanwhile, annual consumer inflation edged up to a three-month high of 0.9% in September from 0.8% in August. Producer price inflation climbed to 5.0% from 3.8%, marking the strongest rise since June 2022. Renewed Middle East hostilities and risks to global oil supplies have added to cost pressures, squeezing corporate margins as businesses struggle to pass higher input costs on to consumers.

China’s trade surplus widened to $133.53 billion in September 2026 from $88.72 billion a year earlier, marking its largest surplus since January 2025. Exports surged 36.3% year-on-year to a record $447.18 billion, while imports rose 31.0% to an all-time high of $313.66 billion. Meanwhile, investors weighed a continued recovery in consumer prices against intensifying cost pressures. 【沙特阿美總裁：全球原油庫存逼近“危險低位”】沙烏地阿拉伯國家石油公司（沙特阿美公司）總裁兼執行長阿明·納賽爾12日在第二十五屆世界石油理事會能源大會（世界石油大會）上表示，全球原油庫存正逼近“極度危險”的低位，可能需要兩年時間才能恢復至正常水平。受霍爾木茲海峽局勢影響，全球能源供應鏈持續承壓，原油供應緊張程度加劇。納賽爾表示，當前商業陸上石油庫存已低於60億桶，這已經是“最後可用的緩衝”，但這些庫存絕大多數實際上無法為市場所用。納賽爾認為，由於全球原油消費需求依然在持續攀升，即便霍爾木茲海峽在短期內完全重新開放、市場信心全面恢復，想要徹底補充消耗殆盡的庫存，至少需要長達兩年的“超負荷加班生產”。

### 2026-10-14 11:20:20  #MKT News #DAX #Impact bearish #EURUSD

【Germany to introduce sugar-sweetened beverage tax in 2027】A proposal to impose a tax on sugar-sweetened beverages has been submitted to the German cabinet; if approved the tax would take effect on July 1, 2027, with small producers exempt. Under the plan, beverages with at least 5 g sugar per 100 ml would be taxed at €0.26 per liter, and those with at least 8 g per 100 ml at €0.32 per liter. Drinks sweetened with non-caloric sweeteners and pure fruit juice would be excluded, as would non-alcoholic beer and wine and beverages made from soy, nuts, seeds or cereals. The government is expected to approve the measure on the 14th. The tax is projected to raise about €690m in 2027 and €925m in 2028.

【State Administration for Market Regulation launches special rectification of saturated‑steam spherical tank risks】China's State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) issued a notice launching a special rectification of risk hazards in saturated‑steam spherical tanks. Design firms for newly manufactured sphere tanks must carry out design‑stage risk assessments based on client conditions, including caustic/alkali stress corrosion, corrosion fatigue, assessment of hazards from saturated‑water flashing and possible explosions, and analysis of instrument impulse‑line freezing risk in low‑temperature use. Required mitigation measures include post‑weld heat‑treatment and weld‑joint hardness testing, periodic testing of in‑tank saturated water quality, automatic internal water‑level control, and optimization of insulation‑layer structural design.

It flagged persistent supply‑chain strains as a risk for further energy and food price rises and warned El Niño could amplify commodity and agricultural disruption. The IMF also identified the AI investment boom as an additional risk: a reversal could compress investment activity and have broader economic spillovers.

### 2026-10-14 11:04:15  #宏觀 #國際

【前三季度 我國與共建“一帶一路”國家進出口超20萬億元】國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會，介紹2026年前三季度進出口情況。近年來，我國與共建“一帶一路”國家經貿合作不斷加深，雙邊貿易規模穩步擴大。今年前三季度，我國對共建國家進出口20.37萬億元，增長17.1%，出口和進口分別增長16.6%、17.9%。主要有三個方面的亮點：一是外貿發展彼此成就。隨著高質量共建“一帶一路”紮實推進，我國和共建國家外貿發展雙向互動，互利共贏趨勢更加明顯。前三季度，我國同近八成的共建國家進出口實現增長。共建國家佔我國進出口總值的比重為51%，對同期我國外貿增長貢獻率達到47.1%。根據最新國際資料測算，前7個月，我國是134個共建國家的前三大貿易伙伴，74個共建國家的最大貿易伙伴。二是跨境物流立體多樣。前三季度，我國以水路運輸方式對共建國家進出口12.99萬億元，增長7.1%，以公路鐵路運輸方式進出口明顯加快，分別增長34.1%和16.4%，以航空運輸方式進出口增長60.1%，在主要運輸方式當中增速領先，中國海關全力支援空中絲綢之路發展，拓展“空空中轉”“空鐵聯運”等多式聯運業務，推廣“空側直通”模式，減少提貨、卸貨、安檢等環節，進一步提升跨境空運運輸效能。三是合作空間持續拓展。從出口來看，我國統籌推進重大標誌性工程，和“小而美”民生專案建設，前三季度，對共建國家承包工程出口近千億元，持續助力重大基礎設施專案建設。對共建國家出口手術機器人增長2.9倍，支援共建國家改善醫療衛生條件。從進口來看，今年以來，中國海關對非洲建交國的幹辣椒、腰果、咖啡豆、野生水產品等陸續實施區域一體准入。前三季度，我國自埃及、塞內加爾、肯亞等國進口農產品均保持兩位數以上增長。上個月，中國海關還准入了塔吉克的李和杏，土耳其的月桂葉，南非的櫻桃等，未來將有越來越多的共建國家優質產品進入我國市場，不斷拓展與共建國家的互利合作空間。

---

【財政部有關負責人前瞻2026年APEC財長會重點議題】第33屆亞太經合組織（APEC）財長會將於10月20日至21日在中國香港舉行。日前，財政部有關負責人介紹了本屆財長會的重點議題、預期成果等相關情況。APEC財長會是APEC機制下最重要的專業部長會之一，是亞太各經濟體加強宏觀經濟政策溝通和財金合作的重要平臺。據介紹，來自21個APEC經濟體的財政部長或其代表、有關國際組織負責人和夥伴方高階代表將參加本屆APEC財長會。本屆APEC財長會將重點討論哪些議題？中方對本屆APEC財長會成果有何期待？今年是中國第三次擔任APEC東道主。“圍繞APEC‘中國年’主題和三大優先領域，結合當前亞太經濟發展面臨的新形勢，我們提出了財金渠道四項重點議題：支援民生髮展的財政政策、數字基礎設施融資、因地制宜促進普惠金融發展、促進APEC青年財經人才交流合作。”這位負責人說。（新華社）

【Customs: China export gains in chips, servers and clean-energy kit supporting global computing and power demand】Customs deputy director Wang Jun said at a state briefing on Oct. 14 that China’s industrial base and tech capacity are bolstering supply to meet rising global computing and power demand. In the first three quarters exports of integrated circuits rose 99.1% YoY; computer parts and accessories increased 2.1-fold; storage components including solid-state drives rose 1.3-fold, helping maintain stable supply of intermediate components in the global compute chain. Exports of servers and data‑processing equipment rose 7.2%, supporting global compute infrastructure. On the power side, first‑three‑quarters exports showed gains in photovoltaic products (+10.3%), wind turbines and parts (+22.4%), power cables (+28.1%), transformers (+22.5%) and various storage batteries (+34.8%); products were shipped to more than 200 countries and regions.

【海關總署：中企為應對全球算力、電力需求增長提供堅實支撐】10月14日，海關總署副署長王軍在國新辦新聞釋出會上表示，我國依託完整產業體系和科技創新能力，為應對全球算力和電力需求增長提供堅實支撐。在算力方面，前三季度我國出口積體電路增長99.1%，電腦零、附件增長2.1倍，固態硬碟等儲存部件增長1.3倍，保障了全球算力產業鏈中間產品的穩定供應。出口伺服器等資料處理裝置增長7.2%，助力全球算力基礎設施建設。在電力方面，前三季度產品出口多點發力。智慧領域，出口光伏產品增長10.3%，風力發電機組及其零件增長22.4%, 持續填補全球清潔能源供給缺口；輸能領域，出口電線、電纜增長28.1%, 變壓器增長22.5%；儲能領域，出口各類蓄電池增長34.8%, 遠銷全球200多個國家和地區。從指甲蓋大小的高速光通訊模組，到成噸重的AI算力伺服器機櫃；從一根家庭用的電線，到遠距離、大容量輸電使用的特高壓換流變壓器；從隨身攜帶的充電寶，到大型工商業儲能裝置；從單體小電池，到集裝箱式整合儲能系統……千千萬萬中國企業，正在為全球算力建設、電網穩定執行、電力安全供應，提供務實可行、成熟可靠的中國產品、中國經驗和中國方案。

【Copper steady as Chile supply concerns offset oil-driven pressure】Copper held steady on Wednesday as worries about Chilean supply risks offset pressure from rising oil. TD Securities said Chile supply risk remains the key focus: strike votes, operational outages and mine-restart developments are sustaining a supply-risk premium, and any escalation in Chile would provide additional near-term support for prices. BHP’s Escondida union said mediation failed and will begin strike action on Wednesday. Centinela and two unions returned to talks on Tuesday to try to resolve a week-long stoppage; unions report operations are being affected. Markets also face tariff uncertainty and ongoing inventory adjustments while higher energy costs keep attention on sticky inflation and slower growth.

【證監會期貨監管司副司長王穎：截至2026年9月末 全市場有效客戶達314.8萬戶】中國證監會期貨監管司副司長王穎10月14日在第19屆國際油脂油料大會暨農畜產業（衍生品）大會上表示，截至2026年9月末，全市場有效客戶達314.8萬戶。商品期貨成交量已連續多年位居世界前列，市場深度和承載力持續增強。目前已上市期貨、期權品種達171個，覆蓋農產品、金屬、能源、化工、航運、金融等國民經濟主要領域，為境內外實體企業提供豐富、高效、便捷的風險管理工具。實體企業已成為參與期貨市場的重要力量。2026年1月至9月，全市場產業客戶日均成交、持倉量分別同比增長3.25%、5.81%，公告參與套期保值的A股非金融類上市公司達1892家，參與率較十年前提升27.2個百分點至35.4%。基差交易、含權貿易等期現結合業務模式廣泛開展。截至目前，期貨市場直接對外開放的特定品種數量達38個，合格境外投資者可參與交易品種達141個。境內期貨交易所陸續在挪威、馬來西亞、日本落地紙漿、豆油、天然橡膠期貨結算價授權專案，實現了“中國價格”走出去，市場反響良好。

US 10-Year Yield Steadies Ahead of CPI Report — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note held steady at around 5.21% on Wednesday as investors awaited September’s consumer inflation report for clues on the Federal Reserve’s next interest rate decision. New York Fed President John Williams reiterated that one more rate hike may be appropriate this year, while further tightening would likely be unnecessary in 2027. Meanwhile, Fed Governor Christopher Waller said inflation expectations remained “well anchored.” Additional remarks from Richmond Fed President Thomas Barkin and Fed Vice Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman are expected later in the day. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 77% probability that the Fed will leave rates unchanged this month, while assigning an 88% chance of a rate hike in December. Treasury yields have retreated in recent sessions as the global bond selloff eased, while heightened volatility in equity markets has boosted demand for bonds as a safe-haven asset.

New principal approvals by the SFC between Jan. 30 and Sep. 30, 2026 rose 65% versus the same period in 2025. Indonesian Shares Rebound — Indonesian equities rose 43 points or 0.7% to 5,968 on Wednesday morning deals, reversing losses in the prior two sessions. Bargain hunters set in after markets fell to a three-month low, while U.S. stock futures strengthened ahead of the inflation data later today. Meanwhile, the IMF raised Indonesia's 2026 GDP growth projection to 5.1% from 5.0%, supported by ongoing efforts to lift household buying power and increased government spending. However, gains were capped by caution ahead of September trade data in main trading partner China. Meanwhile, Chinese producer prices rose the most in over four years in September, as higher fuel prices pushed up costs for businesses. Consumer inflation edged up to a three-month high of 0.9% but slightly below forecasts of 1%. All sectors contributed to the upturn, led by industrials, transport, property, and infrastructure. Among early standouts were Mitra Adiperkasa (4.2%), Medco Energi (3.4%), Bukit Asam (2.8%), and Bank Central Asia (1.7%).

China Posts Biggest Trade Surplus in 20 Months — China’s trade surplus widened to $133.53 billion in September 2026, up from $88.72 billion a year earlier and surpassing forecasts of $118.1 billion. It was the largest trade surplus since January 2025, as exports rose more than imports. Exports jumped 36.30% year-on-year to $447.18 billion, above estimates of 25.5%, as the global AI infrastructure boom continued to sustain strong demand for Chinese high-tech products. Meanwhile, imports jumped 31.0% year-on-year to $313.66 billion, accelerating from a 28.2% surge in August and beating forecasts of 24.5%.

Yen Weakens on Cautious BOJ Rate Outlook — The Japanese yen weakened to around 158.5 per dollar on Wednesday, giving back gains from the previous session after reports suggested the Bank of Japan was reluctant to deliver a rare back-to-back interest rate hike this month, while still considering a possible increase in December. The central bank reportedly sees limited justification for consecutive rate hikes unless there are clear signs of a sharp rise in upside inflation risks. Meanwhile, BOJ Governor Kazuo Ueda said policymakers would continue to closely monitor bond yields, reiterating that long-term interest rates should be determined by market forces. His remarks signaled a cautious approach to monetary policy as the central bank moves further away from strict yield control. The yen also remained under pressure from rising oil prices, as escalating hostilities between Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi rebels heightened concerns over potential supply disruptions in the Middle East.

【中證協：部分券商過於依賴專家諮詢服務】近日，中證協向券商下發了《2025年證券公司釋出證券研究報告業務經營情況統計報告》。《報告》指出，部分券商過於依賴專家諮詢服務。2025年，有59家證券公司研究部門有專家諮詢服務費支出，佔研究所總收入的1.86%，其中專家諮詢服務費支出超10%的有5家公司，高於行業平均水平的有33家。高額專家諮詢服務費表明，部分公司主要依賴透過外部專家提供諮詢服務，而非加強自身研究能力。過度依賴外部專家提供服務，不僅抬升了研究業務成本，壓縮研究部門利潤空間，也導致公司無動力提升自身研究質量。此外，透過外部專家諮詢提供服務，也存在變相利益輸送、不當付費等合規隱患。

### 2026-10-14 10:20:11  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【China Securities Association: Some securities firms overly reliant on external expert consulting】The China Securities Association’s 2025 statistical report on securities firms’ research operations flagged excessive reliance on paid external expert consulting. In 2025, 59 securities firms’ research departments paid expert consulting fees, which accounted for 1.86% of total research revenue; five firms had fees above 10% of research income and 33 firms exceeded the industry average. The association said high expert-fee ratios indicate dependence on external consultants rather than bolstering internal research, raising research unit costs, compressing margins and reducing incentives to improve in-house research quality. It also warned of compliance risks from external expert arrangements, including disguised benefit transfers and improper payments.

### 2026-10-14 10:17:28  #宏觀

【中銀國際：中國新能源車出口退稅率將由13%降至9% 出口增長預計放緩】中銀國際釋出研報稱，中國財政部及國家稅務總局公佈調整部分產品出口退稅政策，新能源車增值稅出口退稅率將由13%降至9%，自2027年4月1日起生效。該行認為，此舉延續光伏及電池等“新三樣”逐步退出政策支援、轉向市場驅動增長的方向。該行指出，新能源車此次由13%降至9%的幅度與光伏、電池首輪調整相同，但過渡期更寬鬆，車企有近六個月準備時間，有助重新調整出口定價、最佳化訂單結構及本地化產能佈局，緩解短期壓力。調整範圍涵蓋乘用及商用新能源車，較市場原先預期的“乘用車先行”更廣;燃油車則不在退稅下調範圍內，有助減輕傳統車企壓力。該行預期，新能源車出口增長將放緩，而非絕對下滑;若車企完全自行消化成本，出口毛利將受壓。中期而言，較低退稅亦會推動車企由出口中國製造車輛轉向海外生產及本地化。競爭力較強、成本控制及海外製造能力較佳的龍頭將更易適應，行業整合或加快。按出口結構，奇瑞汽車(09973)、吉利汽車(00175)、長城汽車(02333)等因燃油車及混動佔比較高而相對受緩衝;小鵬汽車-W(09868)等純新能源出口商則直接壓力較大，惟龍頭仍可透過零售重新定價、供應鏈分擔及加快海外建廠緩解影響。

Hong Kong Stocks Edge Lower — The Hang Seng Index fell 0.2%, or 68 points, to around 24,280 on Wednesday, as losses in financial and technology services stocks outweighed gains in energy, electronic technology and retail shares. Asian chipmakers rebounded following heavy losses, although concerns over AI valuations, Chinese semiconductor competition and the returns on AI infrastructure spending persisted. Brent crude rose to around US$106.60 a barrel amid escalating attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure, heightening concerns over disruptions to Persian Gulf supplies and renewed inflationary pressure. Dollar Holds Decline Ahead of CPI Report — The dollar index hovered around 102.1 on Wednesday after retreating in the previous session, as investors cautiously awaited September’s consumer inflation data for clues on the Federal Reserve’s next interest rate decision. The greenback faced additional pressure after New York Fed President John Williams indicated that one more rate hike may be appropriate this year, while further tightening would likely be unnecessary in 2027. Meanwhile, Fed Governor Christopher Waller said inflation expectations remained “well anchored.” Further commentaries from Richmond Fed President Thomas Barkin and Fed Vice Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman are expected later today. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 77% probability that the Fed will leave rates unchanged this month, while assigning an 88% chance of a rate hike in December.

China Stocks Struggle for Direction — The Shanghai Composite fell 0.3% to 3,791 on Wednesday, while the Shenzhen Component was little changed to around 12,351, as investors weighed signs of a gradual recovery in consumer prices against mounting cost pressures for producers. China’s annual inflation rate edged up to 0.9% in September from 0.8% in August, below expectations of 1% but the highest since June. Producer price inflation accelerated to 5.0% from 3.8%, beating forecasts of 4.5% and marking the strongest rise since June 2022. Renewed hostilities in the Middle East and risks to global oil supplies have intensified price pressures, driving up input costs and squeezing margins as Chinese businesses struggle to pass higher expenses on to consumers. Investors now turn their attention to trade data due later today for further clues on the outlook for external demand. Notable decliners included Foxconn Industrial Internet (-1.4%), Zijin Mining Group (-1.1%), Zhongji Innolight (-2.3%) and Eoptolink Technology (-2.7%).

New Zealand Dollar Gains as US Dollar Rally Pauses — The New Zealand dollar edged up to $0.562 on Wednesday, after gaining 0.5% in the previous session and moving further away from a 17-month low of $0.558, as the US dollar’s advance stalled. Investors are now awaiting US CPI data due later today, along with speeches from Federal Reserve officials, for fresh signals on the outlook for interest rates. Despite the rebound, the kiwi remains vulnerable amid New Zealand’s sluggish economy and widening interest rate differentials with other major economies. Although the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised interest rates at its previous two policy meetings this year, the official cash rate remains below those of several overseas counterparts, reducing the relative appeal of New Zealand dollar-denominated assets to international investors. Political uncertainty ahead of November’s general election also weighed on the currency.

Australian Dollar Hits 2-Week Top — The Australian dollar edged up to around US$0.70, reaching its highest level in more than two weeks as hawkish RBA signals and a broadly weaker US dollar supported the currency. Minutes from the Reserve Bank latest meeting highlighted persistent cost-of-living pressures and the risk of further price increases, keeping the door open for additional rate hikes. Markets are pricing in a 28% chance of another rate increase at the November 3 meeting and about 50% odds of a December move. Focus now turns to employment data due Thursday, with the economy expected to add 20,000 jobs, down from 39,500 in August, while the unemployment rate is forecast to hold steady at 4.6%. Third-quarter CPI data will also take center stage later this month, with core inflation risks remaining elevated above the RBA's 2%–3% target range. Meanwhile, the Aussie drew further support from a weaker US dollar after the Federal Reserve signaled only one more rate hike this year, but no further tightening in 2027.

China Food Prices Fall for 6th Month — China’s food prices declined 1.7% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from a 1.4% drop in the previous month. It was the sixth consecutive month of contraction and the steepest pace in four months. Prices fell further for fresh fruit (-1.5% vs -0.5% in August), fresh vegetables (-8.1% vs -2.8%), edible oils (-1.1% vs -1.3%), and dairy products (-1.5% vs -1.4%). Pork prices, a key component of China’s food basket, continued to decline (-10.0% vs -11.8%), reflecting abundant supply and persistently subdued demand. In contrast, egg prices stayed elevated (14.3% vs 15.0%).

Gains also extended to other heavyweight stocks, including SK Square (1.28%), LG Energy Solution (2.7%), Hyundai Motor (1.1%), Kia Corporation (1.4%), Hyundai Mobis (4.1%), and Samsung Electro-Mechanics (1.7%). Meanwhile, lingering concerns over Chinese chipmaker CXMT's technological advances and the sustainability of AI spending continued to weigh on the sector. ### 2026-10-14 09:36:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

China Producer Inflation Hits Over 4-Year High — China’s annual producer price inflation accelerated to 5.0% in September 2026 from 3.8% in August, exceeding expectations of a 4.5% increase. This marked the strongest reading since June 2022, as higher fuel prices drove up business costs. Prices of production materials accelerated to 6.5% from 5.0%, driven by faster increases in mining (25.4% vs 17.8%), raw materials (9.4% vs 6.7%) and processing (3.4% vs 3.1%). Meanwhile, consumer goods prices fell 0.1%, easing from a 0.5% decline in August, as food prices fell 1.8%, clothing dropped 1.2% and daily-use goods decreased 0.6%, while durable goods prices rose 1.6%. Among major industries, prices increased sharply in coal mining (35.3%), oil and gas extraction (24.5%) and non-ferrous metal smelting (21.4%). On a monthly basis, the PPI rose 1.2%, its biggest increase since April, accelerating from August’s 0.4% gain. In the first nine months of the year, producer prices increased 2.3% compared to the same period last year.

### 2026-10-14 09:35:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

China Inflation Rate Slightly Below Estimates — China’s annual inflation rate edged up to 0.9% in September 2026 from 0.8% in August, slightly below market expectations of 1%. It marked the highest reading since June, with non-food inflation accelerating (1.5% vs 1.2% in August), driven by a sharper rise in transport costs (4.3% vs 2.5%) amid higher gasoline prices and stronger travel demand ahead of the Golden Week break. Upward price pressure also came from clothing (1.2% vs 1.3%), healthcare (2.7% vs 2.7%), and education (1.4% vs 1.4%). In contrast, housing costs stayed subdued (-0.3% vs -0.3%). On the food side, prices declined for the sixth straight month, pointing to the steepest pace in four months (-1.7% vs -1.4%), weighed down by a further drop in pork prices, as abundant pork supplies and weak consumption weighed on prices. Core inflation, excluding food and energy, rose 1.0% yoy, unchanged from August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.2%, below forecasts of 0.3% and slowing from August’s six-month high of 0.4%.

Silver Holds Decline Ahead of US Inflation Data — Silver traded near $59 an ounce on Wednesday after declining for two consecutive sessions, as investors awaited US consumer inflation data for clues on the Federal Reserve’s next policy move. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 77% probability that the Fed will leave interest rates unchanged this month, while assigning an 88% chance of a rate hike in December. Meanwhile, New York Fed President John Williams indicated that one additional rate hike may be warranted this year, but further monetary tightening is unlikely to be necessary in 2027. Geopolitical tensions also remained in focus as oil prices extended gains amid escalating hostilities between Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi rebels, raising concerns over Middle East supply disruptions. Separately, the IMF warned of significant risks to global growth and financial stability stemming from a potential end to the AI investment boom, mounting debt levels, and a prolonged conflict in the Middle East.

### 2026-10-14 09:18:34  #宏觀

【中國代表團完成國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門5D工作組會議參會任務】2026年9月28日至10月8日，國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門第五研究組（SG5）下設5D工作組（WP5D）會議在瑞士日內瓦召開。本次會議是2027年世界無線電通訊大會（WRC-27）研究週期WP5D的第九次會議。WP5D工作組主要負責研究國際行動通訊系統（IMT）地面部分的技術、運營和頻譜相關工作。本次會議聚焦為IMT新增頻段劃分、為手機直連衛星新增頻段劃分、研究未來6G無線技術的提交流程與模板等內容，重點審議WRC-27籌備會議（CPM）報告草案、手機直連衛星系統對IMT系統的干擾保護規則等研究結果。我國提交的2篇CPM相關文稿，以及5篇頻率相容共用研究文稿均被會議採納。來自國務院相關部門，研究機構、高校、企業等12家單位的50餘名代表，以線上線下相結合的方式參會。(工信部)

### 2026-10-14 08:57:58  #MKT News #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Galaxy Securities: Super El Niño formed; near-term soybean meal, oils and white sugar likely firmer】Galaxy Securities says a super El Niño has formed and will inject a forward weather premium, supporting near-term strength in soybean meal, rapeseed meal, the three major vegetable oils and white sugar; corn, hogs and cotton are expected to trade sideways. US soybean downside appears fully priced, which should lift import costs and reinforce soybean meal gains alongside temporary domestic supply tightness; rapeseed meal will track soybean meal. Oils receive three supports: weather premium, stronger crude boosting biodiesel demand, and higher bean-derived costs as US soybean weakness is exhausted. White sugar strength is driven by El Niño-triggered global output risk; raw sugar has firmed, domestic-international spreads narrowed, and Zhengzhou sugar is supported by rising import-cost parity. Corn faces a new-crop supply vs. state-procurement tug-of-war — monitor procurement timing and new-crop opening prices. Hog supply remains loose; a sustained rebound likely needs seasonal pickling/curing demand or concentrated second-cycle restocking to catalyze. Cotton faces new-crop pressure offset by procurement-cost floors; direction will depend on seed-cotton purchase prices, collection progress and whether peak-season orders materialize.

【雄安新區首單“保稅+TIR”業務啟運】據雄安釋出，10月13日，一輛國際公路運輸車駛入雄安綜合保稅區完成裝卸後辦結海關手續，從雄安綜合保稅區卡口駛出。車上裝載近29.79萬元的貨物將經滿洲里公路口岸出境，直達俄羅斯符拉迪沃斯託克。這標誌著雄安新區首單“保稅+TIR（國際公路運輸）”業務正式落地，首次實現了綜合保稅區集貨出口功能與TIR國際公路運輸的疊加。“保稅+TIR”模式將綜合保稅區政策優勢與TIR運輸高效便捷深度融合：企業可將貨物提前存入區內保稅倉庫，根據海外訂單靈活排程、按需集拼發運；出口貨物享受入區退稅，進口貨物入區即保稅，暫緩繳納關稅和進口環節稅。車輛憑TIR證啟運後可實現“一次申報、一證通行、一車直達”，途經各締約國原則上無需重複開箱查驗、換車倒裝。

Australian Stocks Trade Notable Lower — Australian shares dipped 70 points, or 0.8%, to 8,714 in Wednesday morning trade, extending weakness from the prior session. Sentiment weakened after Wall Street closed mostly lower overnight, with earnings from major banks and pressure on memory chip stocks weighing, alongside softer oil prices and persistent Middle East tensions. Locally, caution grew ahead of September labor market data, while minutes from the RBA’s September meeting signaled further hikes remained possible as flagged upside risks materialised. In main trading partner China, CPI/PPI and trade data are due later today. Financials, healthcare, consumer stocks, and services dragged, partly offset by gains in tech and energy minerals. Rio Tinto slipped 0.4% after steady copper output in Q3, with strong Oyu Tolgoi performance offsetting declines elsewhere. The four big banks fell sharply, down 1.4%–2.4%. Other notable laggards included Pro Medicus (-3.4%), Genesis Minerals (-2.5%), and Aristocrat Leisure (-1.9%).

Japanese Shares Rise on Tech Rebound — The Nikkei 225 Index rose 0.4% to above 68,000 on Wednesday, ending a four-session losing streak as selling pressure in technology and AI-related stocks eased. Sentiment also improved following dovish comments from New York Fed President John Williams, who maintained that one additional rate hike would be appropriate this year but saw no need for further tightening in 2027. Lower US Treasury yields provided further support for Japanese equities, although elevated oil prices limited gains. Among the top technology performers, Advantest gained 1%, Kioxia Holdings rose 2.2%, Lasertec advanced 2.5%, SoftBank Group climbed 2.6%, and Taiyo Yuden surged 5.1%. Japanese technology stocks had faced mounting pressure in recent sessions amid concerns over AI-related investments and intensifying competition from China.

【新加坡金管局意外維持政策不變，下調通脹預期】新加坡央行意外維持貨幣政策不變，其預計全球能源緊張與人工智慧帶來的需求熱潮將於明年降溫。新加坡金融管理局不以利率、而以匯率作為主要政策工具。該行週三釋出宣告，維持匯率政策區間的斜率、寬度與中點不變。這是繼4月、7月會議連續多輪緊縮後，首次暫停收緊。媒體調研的20位經濟學家中，僅有3人預測到該決議，多數人原本預期金管局上調政策區間斜率，多數受訪者還預計央行將保留鷹派傾向。該超預期決議公佈後，新加坡元下跌0.1%，報1新元兌1.28美元。澳新銀行亞洲研究主管Khoon Goh表示：“4月與7月連續兩輪緊縮的政策效果仍在傳導，因此央行認為現階段無需進一步收緊。但這不代表緊縮週期結束。若明年通脹上行風險兌現，金管局或將再度收緊政策。”新加坡金管局將今年核心通脹預期區間收窄至1.5%-2%，7月會議的上限預期曾為2.5%；整體通脹預計約2%。

### 2026-10-14 08:15:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Singapore Holds Monetary Policy After Two Tightenings — The Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS) left monetary policy unchanged on Wednesday, pausing after two tightenings earlier this year. The central bank maintained the rate of appreciation of the Singapore dollar nominal effective exchange rate (S$NEER) band, along with its width and midpoint. MAS expects firm near-term growth before moderating as the AI cycle cools and global financial conditions tighten. Meanwhile, core inflation is projected to ease from mid-2027 as imported cost pressures fade and domestic inflation stays subdued. The board narrowed its 2026 core inflation forecast to 1.5–2.0% and sees headline inflation around 2.0%; for 2027, both measures are expected to average 1.5–2.5%. The authority raised the policy band’s slope in April, its first tightening since 2022, as Middle East conflict-driven energy costs lifted imports, and tightened again in July by a smaller margin.

Australia Leading Index Edges Lower — The Westpac–Melbourne Institute Leading Economic Index fell 0.1% month-on-month in September 2026, after remaining flat for the previous two months, marking its first decline since May. Meanwhile, its six-month annualised growth rate held at -0.03%, unchanged from August. The index suggests economic momentum remains slightly below trend heading into late 2026 and early 2027, with growth stabilising at a modest pace rather than stalling. However, higher fuel prices and the RBA’s September rate hike are still filtering through the economy and could weigh on near-term growth. Westpac expects Australia’s economy to expand 1.5% in calendar 2026, below its long-run trend. “For now, it suggests the economy has been stuck in a bit of a holding pattern over the last two years,” said Matthew Hassan, Westpac’s head of Australian macro-forecasting.

### 2026-10-14 08:07:46  #MKT News #Commodities #Precious Metals #XAUUSD #Impact bullish

【CITIC Securities: Gold price floor may lift; gold stocks likely to lead】CITIC Securities says ETF inflows and central-bank buying may already have lifted gold's floor. The firm views the medium-term outlook as constructive: if US‑Iran tensions ease before the US midterms, improved oil-price expectations could create a predictable window for easing market tightening expectations and slow the rise in US Treasury yields. That dynamic, together with reduced futures selling and continued ETF buying, would support gold price repair. On the sector, using a $4,000/oz 2026 floor estimate CITIC calculates a gold-sector PE trough around 15x; under a $4,200/oz assumption the sector's 2026/2027 PEs are about 15x/12x, implying the valuation bottom could be re‑tested. Gold equities typically lead bullion on the upside; CITIC's gold index has already rallied over 20% from its trough, so a stabilizing gold price would be a key signal for a broader sector rebound.

【India central bank in crisis mode defending rupee; policy-error risk rises】Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has apparently entered crisis mode to halt a rupee free-fall, reactivating tools used during the 2013 taper tantrum. In under three months the RBI pushed banks to raise about $133bn from non-resident Indians; when that programme ended in August without reversing bearish sentiment, authorities last weekend raised lenders’ FX hedging costs and ordered oil importers to source dollars domestically, bypassing market exchange rates. The more authorities defend the psychologically important 1 USD = 100 INR threshold, the greater the risk of costly over-intervention. USD/INR has traded around 95. Brent is above $100/bbl amid Middle East tensions, food-driven inflation is at a two-year high and surging US Treasury yields continue to drain EM flows, making the currency defence potentially unsustainable.

【摩根大通上調第四季度、明年第一季度布倫特現貨原油價格預期】摩根大通在研報中稱，將2026年第四季度及2027年第一季度布倫特現貨原油預測價格上調5美元/桶。現第四季度預測價105美元/桶；明年第一季度預測價100美元/桶。分析師Martijn Rats等人表示，儘管中東出口量有所上升，但包含供應至本土煉油廠在內的整體市場供應量，仍遠低於去年水平。此外，陸上原油庫存持續下降，處於多年低位。油輪襲擊事件再度增多，過去兩週約每週11起，而7、8月每週不足4起。“針對沙特基礎設施的襲擊有所增加。原油出口仍在繼續，但相關不確定性居高不下。”運費上漲也對布倫特油價形成支撐。“相比仍需用來滿足需求的長距離運輸原油，布倫特原油輸往西北歐具備10至15美元/桶的成本優勢。”

【新能源汽車增值稅出口退稅率將降至9% 外貿“新三樣”進入體系化競爭階段】10月12日，隨著財政部、稅務總局釋出公告，中國新能源汽車出口的退稅紅利退坡正式進入倒計時。根據公告，自2027年4月1日起，新能源汽車增值稅出口退稅率由13%下調至9%，新能源汽車出口消費稅退稅政策維持不變。退稅率判定以出口貨物報關單註明的出口日期為準——這意味著，哪怕銷售合同簽訂日期鎖定今年，明年4月1日之後報關出口的車輛，也需執行新標準。4個百分點，放在中國新能源汽車出口數千億元的大盤子面前，是一道足以重塑行業利潤結構的算術題。有車企人士受訪表示，會利用視窗期重新核算出口全鏈路成本，透過調整產品結構等方式贏得主動。繼光伏、鋰電池之後，新能源汽車政策漸回中性。業內認為，出口退稅退坡，退的是政策，進的是能力。中國汽車出海，將從政策紅利、成本優勢的疊加期，轉向產品、品牌、本地化運營的綜合比拼；而外貿“新三樣”，也將集體進入“體系化競爭”新階段。

【人民日報：中歐該打造怎樣的“夥伴邏輯”？】對歐洲而言，僅靠保護性措施和強化監管，換不來持久競爭力。國際能源署釋出的《2026年電力報告》顯示，2025年歐盟能源密集型工業的電價仍超過美國兩倍，比中國高近50%。過度依賴外部能源供應、電網升級滯後、碳定價成本轉嫁不暢——歐洲能源轉型中的結構性問題，使其綠色雄心與實際競爭力之間出現裂痕。歐方若不能正視在創新轉化率、單一市場一體化、能源成本和監管負擔等方面的短板，僅將目光投向外部競爭者，恐將錯失自我革新的視窗。 面對中國科技進步，歐洲不應糾結於風險與機遇孰多孰少的靜態演算法。能否化“風險”為機遇，既考驗自身改革魄力，也取決於如何看待合作伙伴。真正的夥伴邏輯，是承認彼此的進步可以成為共同機遇，以穩定、互惠的行動積累信任。唯有如此，合作訴求才能真正轉化為共同發展的現實。

### 2026-10-14 07:22:45  #其他

【人民時評：以投資最佳化支撐產業升級】當前投資有所回落，是傳統投資模式向新型投資模式轉換的體現，是結構性調整與政策有序銜接的產物，而非潛在有效投資空間的收窄。從中長期視角看，我國有效投資空間仍然巨大，新增長點正在加速形成。從細分行業看，今年1至8月，積體電路製造業投資增長12.0%，鋰離子電池製造業投資增長20.6%，電子電路製造業投資增長58.8%。這些資料表明，投資並未全面收縮，而是在經歷從傳統領域向新興領域的重心轉移、從規模擴張向效能提升的深刻轉換。在這一過程中，新基建與AI（人工智慧）投資尤為關鍵。算力、資料、模型、應用等環節相互牽引，既能夠創造當期需求，又提升長期供給效率，是促進新一輪有效投資的重要引擎。當前投資領域的調整，正是從“有沒有”向“好不好”、從“大不大”向“強不強”躍升的必經階段。有“六張網”建設的萬億級投資勢能，有新基建+AI投資的廣闊空間，有投資於物和投資於人緊密結合的協同效應，我國投資的巨大潛力將充分釋放，既惠及當下增長，更支撐長遠發展。（人民日報）

【KKR: Bond sell-off offers chance to lock Asian high-yield-like returns while avoiding junk credit risk】KKR said the global bond-market sell-off presents an opportunity for fixed-income investors to lock yields comparable to Asian high-yield while avoiding the credit risks of junk bonds. Dengzhao Pan, head of Asia liquid credit at KKR, said: "We view this as an opportunity to upgrade portfolio quality rather than to sit on the sidelines. Improving credit quality can provide a larger margin of safety and reduce expected credit losses." KKR reported $796 bln AUM at end-June. The firm said Middle East conflict-driven oil-price moves and ensuing central-bank tightening have pushed investment-grade corporate yields, including Asian credits, to their highest in over two years, bringing some high-quality bonds' yields close to typical junk levels. Asian investment-grade USD bonds yield 5.8% on average, with longer-dated and subordinated issues yielding more.

【美聯儲理事庫克稱無法出席白宮11月5日聽證 要求推遲至12月議息會議後】美聯儲理事麗莎·庫克表示，她無法出席白宮11月5日舉行的聽證會，該聽證會是美國總統唐納德·特朗普就針對庫克的房貸欺詐指控發起的新調查的一部分。庫克的律師Abbe Lowell週二在致白宮的信中表示，屆時庫克將“在歐洲參加金融和貨幣政策會議，履行其作為美聯儲理事的公務”。Lowell寫道，為讓庫克有足夠時間準備，並協調其他日程衝突，聽證會必須安排在美聯儲12月8日至9日會議之後。特朗普上週簽署備忘錄，宣佈將成立委員會，調查他有關庫克實施房貸欺詐的指控。他表示，庫克必須前往白宮出席聽證會，為自己進行辯護。這項調查標誌著特朗普罷免庫克行動的最新升級。

【權益法適用門檻或提升，銀行股廉價購買利得不易得】近期，財政部起草了《企業會計準則解釋第22號（徵求意見稿）》，並向相關單位徵求意見。徵求意見稿進一步細化長期股權投資權益法的適用條件和權益法下廉價購買利得的列報，提出表決權比例低於20%情形下重大影響的認定標準，投資方需要同時滿足四項條件才可使用權益法核算。在當前A股銀行股大面積破淨的市場環境下，廉價購買利得可給投資方帶來可觀的營業外收入。若相關規則最終落地，雖然險企、AMC（金融資產管理公司）配置銀行股的底層邏輯不會改變，但權益法核算門檻將會提高，獲取廉價購買利得的難度將加大。（中證報）

【美國銀行因反洗錢合規疏漏面臨12億美元罰款】美國銀行因違反反洗錢及制裁規定被美國監管機構處以逾12.1億美元罰款。監管機構稱，該行為保護自身營收，“實際上外包了”合規工作的關鍵環節。美國財政部週二宣佈的執法行動包括美國貨幣監理署(OCC)處以創紀錄的7.5億美元民事罰款。財政部外國資產控制辦公室(OFAC)表示，與美國銀行達成的和解涉及475起明顯違反制裁規定的行為，涉及俄羅斯和伊朗等國家。監管機構還對該行下達同意令。美國銀行定於週三開盤前公佈第三季度業績。該行表示，預計業績中將計入12億美元不可抵稅的費用，使淨利潤摺合每股減少約0.17美元。監管機構稱，該行“優先考慮自身營收，並儘量壓低履行合規要求所需的資源投入”，轉而依靠執法機構的調查以及其他銀行提出的請求來監控客戶。美國財政部在宣告中表示：“該行實際上將主動發現和報告義務的很大一部分外包給了第三方。”

NZX 50 Extends Losses, Tracking Wall Street Lower — The NZX 50 dropped 48 points, or 0.4%, to 13,702 in Wednesday morning trade, extending losses from the previous session and tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight, weighed down by semiconductor heavyweights. Caution mounted ahead of the release of China's September CPI, PPI, and trade data later today amid weak domestic demand and rising oil prices. Traders also awaited US inflation data for clues about the Fed's interest rate decision at its upcoming meeting, as well as New Zealand's food inflation and business PMI data due later this week. Traders continued to monitor the war in the Middle East, including the Saudi Arabia-Houthi conflict and its impact on oil prices. Financials, energy, technology, and consumer discretionary stocks mainly weighed on the index, with notable losses from AFT Pharmaceuticals (-2.0%), Infratil (-1.8%), Briscoe Group (-1.8%), Freightways Group (-1.5%), Summerset Group (-1.2%), Meridian Energy (-0.9%), Channel Infrastructure (-0.9%), and A2 Milk (-0.6%).

【跌破11萬元/噸，碳酸鋰價格又到“十字路口”，儲能或成需求增長核心引擎】10月13日，碳酸鋰期貨主力合約LC2701下跌1.67%，報收107060元/噸，較前一個交易日11.79%的跌幅有所收窄。據統計，9月以來LC2701累計跌幅達到33.5%，一度創下102700元/噸的年內最低價。現貨方面，Mysteel資料顯示，10月13日電池級碳酸鋰（晚盤）均價較前一日再降2900元，報價108850元/噸。“碳酸鋰庫存增多”“儲能需求不及預期”“供給又要過剩”……近期碳酸鋰行業似乎較為低迷。有長期跟蹤碳酸鋰產業人士直言，9月以來碳酸鋰價格持續走弱更多是訊息面擾動疊加期貨市場資金博弈，現貨供需關係並非那麼寬鬆。總體來看，市場對碳酸鋰的“擔憂”主要還是來自儲能需求潛在下滑風險。業內普遍認為，儲能是拉動碳酸鋰需求增長的核心引擎。有分析認為，考慮到高收益水平容量電價有效視窗期只有2年至3年，預計2026年、2027年儲能裝機將保持高增長。（上證報）

New Zealand Tourist Arrivals Rise Least in 3 Months — Visitor arrivals to New Zealand climbed 8.0% year-on-year to 248,714 in August 2026, slowing from an 8.5% rise in July and marking the smallest growth in three months. Arrivals from China increased by 6,500, followed by Australia (up 5,800), the US (up 2,000), and Malaysia (up 1,300). There were record arrivals for an August month of overseas visitors from some markets, including Australia (125,600), Taiwan (5,800), and Singapore (4,600). Overall, overseas visitor arrivals reached 99% of pre-pandemic levels, compared with 251,100 in August 2019. Australians remained the largest source of visitors, accounting for 51% of the total, followed by China (11%), the US (6%), and Japan (4%), highlighting the continued recovery in international tourism demand.

【Cook's lawyer condemns Trump's new probe】The New York Times reports Fed Governor Cook is disputing President Trump's latest effort to remove her and has questioned the objectivity of a White House investigation into alleged mortgage fraud. In a letter to the White House, Cook's lawyer Abe Lowell said she cannot attend a hearing scheduled for Nov. 5 because she will be in Europe for a finance and monetary policy meeting, and that any hearing would be held no earlier than after the Fed's Dec. 8-9 policy meeting. Lowell also challenged the probe's neutrality, noting the committee conducting it is composed of Trump-appointed political officials, including White House National Economic Council director Hassett. Trump has long pushed to increase control over the Fed to lower borrowing costs; when asked last Friday about the probe he called Cook "dishonest" and said he would "absolutely" fire her if the committee found grounds for removal.

Argentina Inflation Rises in September — Argentina’s consumer price index rose 1.9% month-over-month in September 2026, up from 1.7% in August, and accumulated 23.6% year-to-date. Housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels recorded the largest monthly increase at 3.1%, followed by education at 3.0%. Restaurants and hotels posted the smallest increase at 1.1%, while clothing and footwear rose 1.4%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages made the largest contribution to monthly inflation across regions, except in Greater Buenos Aires, where housing and utilities had the greatest impact. Core CPI rose 1.7%, driven in part by higher prices for dining out and pharmaceutical products. Seasonal prices increased 2.9%, led by vegetables, tubers and legumes, while regulated prices rose 2.3%, reflecting higher gas, electricity and private healthcare costs.

Ibovespa Drops as Inflation Forecast Rises — The Ibovespa fell 1% to close at 206,887 on Tuesday, weighed down by losses in major banks, utilities and mining stocks. The Focus survey raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.22% from 5.01%, marking a fourth consecutive increase, while the Selic benchmark rate remained unchanged at 13.25%. The IMF also cut its 2026 GDP growth forecast for Brazil to 2.2% from 2.4%, citing a slowdown expected in 2026–2027 due to restrictive interest rates aimed at bringing inflation back to target, the withdrawal of fiscal stimulus and global uncertainty. Major banks declined, with Itaú falling 1.6% and Bradesco losing 1%. Utilities also retreated, with Axia down 1.6% and Sabesp shedding 3.1%. Mining stocks weakened as ample global supply, high Chinese inventories and lower shipping costs continued to weigh on iron ore prices. Vale fell 1.1%. In contrast, Petrobras gained 1.5% as oil prices rebounded.

【COLLINS: Further rate hikes this year may be needed; watch for inflation broadening】Boston Fed President COLLINS said policy rates may need to rise further before year-end to curb inflation, and any additional hikes and timing will depend on incoming economic data. She expects US growth to continue and the labor market to remain near full employment; under that baseline, productivity gains should help rebalance demand and supply and bring inflation toward 2%. She warned two other scenarios are nearly as likely: AI-driven investment could spur stronger-than-expected spending, overheating the labor market and keeping inflation above target; and tariffs or higher energy prices—especially if conflict involving Iran continues—could sustain price pressure. COLLINS said it is important to watch whether high inflation remains concentrated in energy and AI-infrastructure sectors or begins to broaden.

Brent Crude Prices Rise as Supply Risks Mount — Brent crude pared earlier losses to trade over $106.5 a barrel on Tuesday amid concerns over supply risks linked to the conflict between Saudi Arabia and the Iran-backed Houthis. The militants have stepped up attacks on Saudi Arabia since last week, targeting energy facilities and airports. The Houthis attacked King Khalid International Airport in Riyadh on Saturday, while a Saudi-led coalition said it intercepted a ballistic missile fired toward the capital on Monday. Meanwhile, Iran is fortifying sensitive sites, including nuclear facilities, and rallying supporters amid concerns over possible renewed US attacks after the November midterm elections. In contrast, prospects for additional Russian energy supplies to global markets limited oil’s gains. An agreement would allow Russia to supply diesel to the US and other markets for six months. However, Goldman Sachs said the additional Russian diesel supplies were unlikely to significantly ease pressure on global fuel prices.

【Bank of America fined $1.2bn by US Treasury for AML, sanctions failures】The US Treasury fined Bank of America (BAC.N) $1.2bn for “serious and persistent” anti‑money‑laundering and sanctions compliance failures, saying the bank failed to report suspicious transactions totaling more than $5bn. Regulators required Bank of America to enter a consent order that mandates third‑party remediation to identify and report previously undetected suspicious transactions. The action is a regulatory hit ahead of the bank’s upcoming Q3 results.

### 2026-10-14 04:14:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Uruguay Industrial Production Rises in August — Uruguay’s industrial production rose 4.8% year over year in August 2026. The increase was driven primarily by a 136.1% surge in petroleum refining, contributing 3.8 percentage points to overall growth, partly reflecting a low base as ANCAP’s refinery was offline for repairs during part of August 2025. Food products manufacturing rose 5.4%, contributing 2.3 percentage points, while paper and paper products increased 4.4%. In contrast, beverage manufacturing slumped 30.5%, making the largest negative contribution, followed by computer, electronic and optical products (-90.1%) and chemicals (-5.6%). Meanwhile, the Index of Hours Worked rose 0.3%, while the Employment Index fell 0.9%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production increased 0.4%. Year to date, output rose 3.3%.

### 2026-10-14 03:20:17  #市場

【美債期貨負凸性升至近20年高位 MOVE指數創立者警告“噩夢情景”】美債市場MOVE指數的創立者Harley Bassman警告稱，債市一個鮮受關注的特徵可能令拋售行情更為劇烈，並進一步推高債券收益率。Bassman在其Convexity Maven通訊中發文稱，這一風險源於期貨合約結算時，交易員提供交割債券的方式。他說，近期收益率大幅躍升對較長期債券的打擊最大，期限最長的債券根據所謂的“最便宜可交割”(cheapest-to-deliver)規則成為用於履行期貨合約交割義務的債券。但這些債券的久期也更高，這意味著利率上升時，它們面臨更大的下跌風險。為了對沖這種風險，基金經理可能會在合約到期前賣出其他長期債券，從而形成一種在價格已經下跌時進一步放大賣壓的機制。Bassman表示，與債券期貨相關的負凸性，也就是其異常突出的下行風險，已經升至2007年以來最高水平，債市最近一輪拋售背後可能有其推波助瀾。

### 2026-10-14 03:16:39  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500

【Dalio: US bond bear market far from over; supply-demand imbalance to push yields higher】Bridgewater founder Ray Dalio said the US bond sell-off is far from over as rising government and corporate funding needs and weaker demand from some traditional buyers intensify pressure on Treasuries. He warned the US is entering a phase in which higher bond supply will push yields up, and rising borrowing costs will further enlarge debt — a cycle that would only end if high rates weaken the economy and financial markets enough to rebalance supply and demand. Dalio said US debt is at its highest level since WWII and is likely to worsen in coming years. He noted the government expects to roll about $10 tln of maturing debt this year and to add roughly $2.1 tln in new borrowing. He cautioned demand from major foreign Treasury holders could weaken while corporate bond and equity issuance to fund AI infrastructure competes with Treasuries for capital. He also said signs in corporate credit show investors are demanding higher returns to absorb new supply. Given rising supply and weakening demand, Dalio said shorting Treasuries remains more attractive than going long.

Heating Oil Surges Nearly 5% — Heating oil futures in the US surged to $4.92 per gallon on Tuesday, the highest in three weeks and close to last month's record high of $5.30, as unprecedented supply tightness returned to the market's forefront. The strikes erased already-dim expectations that the energy ceasefire touted by President Trump would hold, stoking fears of continued attacks on major Russian refineries. The developments also deepened doubts that Russia would release 36 million barrels of diesel for export, a pledge that faced skepticism upon announcement, as a domestic distillate shortage prompted the Moscow to halt exports last quarter. Gasoline Holds Decline — US gasoline futures held near $3.25 a gallon, extending their downward trend since October 9th as traders continue to weigh the impact of fuel supplies from Russia. While Russia had agreed to supply diesel to global markets to help lower fuel prices, experts believe the deal announced by President Trump to be very small, equal to just about 10 days of total US demand or slightly more than a single day of global demand. Prospects of a Russia–Ukraine energy ceasefire also remain in focus as Russia continued to attack Ukrainian energy facilities, contrary to President Trump's claims of an immediate truce. Both Ukraine and Germany have pushed for strike bans to include Ukrainian electricity infrastructure as well as Russian refineries. Attacks on tankers crossing the Strait of Hormuz also remain risks to feedstock supply. Against this backdrop, pump prices remain elevated, with 12.1% of global refining capacity offline as of September, although prices have eased in recent weeks.

ASML slid 4.2% ahead of its earnings report this week, tracking sharp losses for chip producers across major Western economies after Chinese chipmaker CXMT said it achieved a breakthrough in its memory architecture that rivals Korean and US competitors. FTSE 100 Halts 2-Day Gain — The FTSE 100 pared earlier gains and edged down on Tuesday, after two days of gains, as renewed concerns over the sustainability of the artificial intelligence rally weighed on global markets after the US open. The pullback in chipmakers overshadowed strong earnings from major US banks, although London’s limited exposure to technology stocks helped it outperform several European peers. London Stock Exchange Group fell nearly 5% after reports emerged that it was in discussions to acquire credit information platform Octus from UK private equity firm Permira, a development the company confirmed. Mining shares also came under pressure, with Antofagasta dropping 5%, Rio Tinto losing 2% and Anglo American declining 1.6%. BP provided some support, rising more than 3% as investors responded to the company’s progress in reducing its debt burden. The oil major cut net debt by approximately $8 billion over the past three months, highlighting a significant improvement in its balance sheet.

### 2026-10-13 23:59:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures at 1-Month High — Cocoa prices rose further to around $5,800 a tonne, their highest level since mid-September, supported by weather-related supply concerns. Local reports indicated that heavy rainfall in the Ivory Coast, the world’s largest cocoa producer, had disrupted harvesting and delayed bean deliveries to ports, limiting near-term availability. However, both the Ivory Coast and Ghana have received below-average rainfall in recent months, raising the risk of stress on the next crop. Local farmers remained worried about the size and quality of the next cocoa crop, which is expected to be harvested from early 2027. The possible strengthening of El Niño adds to these concerns, as it could bring hotter, drier weather to West Africa and increase the risk of heavy rain and flooding in parts of Latin America, including Ecuador. Meanwhile, third-quarter cocoa-grinding data from Europe and North America, due on October 15, will provide further insight into demand.

US Stocks Erase Gains — US stock indices pulled back from session highs on Tuesday as Treasuries reversed gains and pressured AI infrastructure names alongside major earnings. Chipmakers extended declines on reports that China's CXMT advanced new memory architecture, threatening US and Korean rivals. Broadcom CEO Hock Tan also warned of excessive AI lab spending yielding poor returns, sending Micron, Marvell, and SanDisk down 4% to 6%. In turn, J&J sank 5.5% on soft sales, while UNH gained 2% on an upgraded profit outlook. Financials saw uneven results, with Citigroup falling 2% despite posting strong revenues and Wells Fargo slipping 1.4% after publishing results. On the other hand Goldman Sachs jumped 1% on a boost from equity trading. JPMorgan rose 1.5% as a raised net interest income forecast offset weaker fixed-income trading and higher costs.

### 2026-10-13 23:27:48  #MKT News #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Strait of Hormuz shipping risk rises as more than a dozen VLCCs turn back near Fujairah】Iranian attacks on vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz have surged, potentially threatening the recent rebound in Persian Gulf oil exports. Shipping sources say cargo volumes via the strait earlier this month recovered close to pre-conflict levels, but recent signs point to a possible slowdown. There is no clear evidence that crude loading at several major producers has changed, and one owner said ship transits appear unrestricted. Aggregated tanker-tracking data show more than a dozen VLCCs and several Suezmaxes turned back or altered course near Fujairah, a major hub at the entrance to the strait. Brokers and some owners report additional slowdowns, notably longer wait times for ship-to-ship transfers, though an STS operator said his business had not been affected.

【Colombia central bank signals hawkish bias: rates likely to stay high long-term, further hikes not ruled out】Colombia central bank co-director Mauricio Villamizar said on Tuesday policy rates may need to rise further and remain higher for longer to curb rising inflation risks. Speaking at the IMF annual meeting in Bangkok, Villamizar cited El Nifio, the Iran war and a large fiscal deficit as upside price pressures, saying El Nifio’s hit to food and energy could exceed market expectations and push the country into "unknown territory." He said the bank expects rates to stay elevated for an extended period but that further hikes are on the table if necessary. The remarks follow a surprise rate increase last month that pushed rates to their highest level so far in 2024 as the central bank seeks to rebuild policy credibility after years of missing inflation targets.

【高盛分析師電話會要點總結】 
1、CEO蘇德巍短期內不會卸任，用他自己的話說：“高盛目前沒有正在進行中的繼任計劃、交接安排或時間表。”

2、這位高盛CEO顯然在認真思考人工智慧（AI），既看到機遇，也看到風險。他認為高盛在這波大規模AI投資浪潮中處於有利位置，並稱高盛是“唯一身處AI融資中心”的金融機構。

3、併購、IPO和其他融資活動並未消亡，儘管節奏有所放緩。宏觀和利率環境較為複雜，本季度也確實有一些IPO未能成行，但蘇德巍表示併購儲備依然強勁，“資本市場活動仍然良好”。

4、蘇德巍在電話會上表示，高盛會考慮在資產管理領域進行另一次收購——該領域近期已取得不少進展——但前提是財務條件合適，且更重要的是，必須與高盛文化契合。

Azerbaijan CPI Rises the Most in 8 Months — The CPI in Azerbaijan increased 0.9% month-over-month in September 2026, the most in eight months, following a 0.2% rise in August. Prices for food, alcoholic beverages and tobacco products went up 1.4%, with cost for buckwheat, beef, mutton and chicken, fresh and frozen fish, eggs, plums, peaches, chestnuts, greens, cucumbers, tomatoes, sweet peppers, eggplant, green beans, carrots, onions and potatoes rising. Prices for non-food products increased 0.4%, led by clothing, paint, sawn wood, furniture, laptops and tablet computers while prices for jewelry decreased. Year-on-year, the CPI rose 5.5%, slightly less than 5.7% in August.

US 10-Year Yield Erases Decline — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note erased its early pullback on Tuesday to hover above the 5.2% threshold, as markets continued to assess the magnitude of inflation risks from higher energy prices and the outlook for bond issuance by the tech sector. Energy benchmarks recovered in the session as the tight supply backdrop is likely to persist. Signals that Saudi Arabia would strike Houthi targets prolonged the conflict that jeopardizes tanker flows through the Red Sea, while evidence of low oil loadings at Russian Black Sea ports erased any signals that Russia would step up energy exports. Still, improvements in Chinese AI chip technology raised concerns of foreign competition for domestic semiconductor giants, limiting the outlook for corporate credit. The latest research showed industry players penning $4.1 trillion in AI-related debt to be issued by 2030, crowding out the Treasury in its issuance of new securities.

Slowing earnings growth alone may not halt the rally, but with US equities near record highs and growing concern that inflation could be stickier than expected, companies will need materially strong results to lift the market further. With expectations so elevated, even slight misses could trigger strong market reactions."

### 2026-10-13 22:38:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Rise to Nearly 3-Week High — US natural gas prices climbed above $3.20 per million British thermal units on Tuesday, reaching their highest level since September 24, when prices touched $3.37, driven by lower production. Average output across the Lower 48 states fell to 110.6 billion cubic feet per day so far in October, down from record levels of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The decline reflects production shutdowns caused by Hurricane Isaias, along with pipeline disruptions and force majeure incidents across several states. Daily output was expected to drop to around 107.1 bcfd on Tuesday, an eight-month low, although offshore production in the Gulf of Mexico is gradually recovering. By Monday, only 25% of offshore gas output remained shut in, compared with a peak of 59% on October 9. Meanwhile, LNG feedgas deliveries averaged 17.4 bcfd in October, below September’s 17.9 bcfd. Forecasts for milder weather and weaker demand over the next two weeks could limit further price gains.

### 2026-10-13 22:24:04  #MKT News #Commodities #Industrial Metals #Copper #Impact bullish

【Escondida labour dispute escalates; over 1,000 workers may strike from Wednesday】Wage mediation between unions and operator BHP at Chile’s Escondida, the world’s largest copper mine, entered its final day after BHP revised its pay offer. Workers at an Antofagasta-owned copper unit have also returned to talks. Copper’s proximity to record highs has driven demands for greater profit sharing while miners push to contain cost inflation, stoking labour tensions and raising the risk of further supply disruption. BHP said its latest proposal would keep Escondida supervisors and staff among the highest paid in the sector; unions said it answered some demands but significant gaps remain. Union leader Alexis Barrera said a proposed signing bonus of about $25,500 per worker, presented as part of an overall benefits package, is below market given the company’s large profits. If no agreement is reached on Tuesday, more than 1,000 union members could start striking from Wednesday; mediation could also be extended.

【俄羅斯原油出口連續承壓 黑海港口發運放緩拖累整體供應】由於黑海地區安全擔憂加劇，俄羅斯主要黑海港口的運營再次放緩，導致該國原油出口量下降。彙總的油輪追蹤資料顯示，截至10月11日的四周內，俄羅斯海運原油出口量降至每日370萬桶，較截至10月4日四周的修正後資料減少11萬桶。俄羅斯新羅西斯克港Sheskharis石油碼頭的衛星影象顯示，油輪在主要原油裝載泊位停靠的時間長達四天，是同等規模油輪在俄羅斯波羅的海港口裝貨時間的兩倍，導致該港原油發運明顯放緩。上週初，一艘滿載原油的油輪在駛離新羅西斯克港後遭無人機襲擊，進一步加劇了船東和船員對該港裝貨安全的擔憂。此外，莫斯科已將所有哈薩克原油出口轉移至Sheskharis碼頭，為俄羅斯自身原油在波羅的海烏斯季盧加港的出口騰出泊位。不過，波羅的海港口出口量的增長尚未完全抵消黑海港口出口量的下降。

### 2026-10-13 22:22:56  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Russian crude exports under pressure as Black Sea loadings slow】Russian seaborne crude exports averaged 3.70 mln bpd in the four weeks to Oct 11, down 110k bpd from the four weeks to Oct 4, shipping-tracking data show. Operations at Black Sea ports have slowed amid heightened security concerns: satellite imagery of Novorossiysk’s Sheskharis terminal shows tankers occupying major loading berths for up to four days—about twice the loading time seen at comparable Baltic ports—reducing shipments. A fully laden tanker was struck by a drone after leaving Novorossiysk earlier last week, heightening owners’ and crews’ safety concerns. Russian authorities have rerouted all Kazakh crude exports through Sheskharis to free Ust-Luga berths for Russian cargoes, but higher Baltic shipments have not fully offset the Black Sea shortfall.

【High rates fail to curb lending; U.S. big-three banks post record net interest income】Despite higher rates, consumer and corporate borrowing kept pace, lifting JPMorgan, Citigroup and Wells Fargo to a combined Q3 net interest income of $56.5bn, the fifth consecutive quarter of record NII, results published Tuesday showed. JPMorgan said it expects net interest income to increase further in 2027, a contrast with earlier industry assumptions of falling rates when record trading revenue had drawn investor focus. All three banks reported declines in nonaccrual loans and said borrowers broadly remain able to service debt. BofA will report Q3 results on Wednesday. Banks said they remain cautious amid geopolitical risks in Ukraine and the Middle East, persistent inflation and elevated oil prices.

TSX Edges Higher as Bond Yields Stabilize — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged higher to above 35,500 on Tuesday as bond yields stabilized. Oil prices halted their rebound after US President Trump said Russia could resume diesel exports to the US. This supported credit-sensitive sectors such as banking. RBC rose more than 0.5%, while TD Bank, BMO, Scotiabank and CIBC gained about 1% each. Energy producers also advanced despite the pause in the oil rally, as prices remained elevated, with Canadian Natural, Suncor and Imperial Oil up about 3% each. Gold prices pared earlier gains, with Agnico Eagle, Barrick and WPM shedding around 1% each. Elsewhere, CSG announced it had acquired a 9.5% stake in MDA Space, becoming the company’s largest shareholder; MDA traded near the flatline. In technology, Shopify jumped 4% and Constellation Software rose 3.5% amid gains in US hyperscalers and other software stocks, while Celestica fell 7%, tracking losses among chipmakers.

### 2026-10-13 22:16:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Falls on Inflation and GDP Outlook — The Ibovespa fell over 0.5% to hover below 208,000 on Tuesday, weighed down by losses in major banks, utilities and mining stocks. The Focus survey raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.22% from 5.01%, marking a fourth consecutive increase, while the Selic benchmark rate remained unchanged at 13.25%. The IMF also lowered its growth projections for Brazil, cutting its 2026 GDP growth estimate to 2.2% from 2.4%. The fund attributed the slowdown expected between 2026 and 2027 to restrictive interest rates aimed at bringing inflation back to target, the withdrawal of fiscal stimulus and global uncertainty. Major banks declined, with Itaú down nearly 1.5% and Bradesco losing about 1%. Utilities also fell, with Sabesp down nearly 2%. Miners weakened as abundant global supply, elevated Chinese inventories and lower shipping costs continued to pressure iron ore prices. Vale shed nearly 1%.

### 2026-10-13 22:09:08  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Microsoft #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #MSFT.O #Impact bullish #Nasdaq100 #S&P500

【Microsoft rallies 50% since June, market value up $1.5tn; near record close】Microsoft (MSFT.O) has rallied more than 50% from its June low, adding roughly $1.5 trillion in market value and is poised to close at a record high for the first time in nearly a year. The fourth consecutive monthly advance has accelerated as investor confidence in Microsoft’s AI competitiveness improved. The stock was among the S&P 500’s worst performers in June, plunging 17% in its worst month since 2000 and erasing about $600 billion of market value. The reversal began after the July 29 earnings release, when cloud revenue growth hit its fastest pace in four years; the shares jumped 16% the next day and Microsoft posted its strongest quarterly performance since 1998. At least two firms have since upgraded the stock to buy, citing confidence in Microsoft’s ability to capture rising AI services demand.

【US existing-home sales rise in September; mortgage-rate surge likely to curb year-end activity】US existing-home sales rose 1.5% in September to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 3.99 mln, the first monthly increase in four months and slightly above economists' 3.95 mln forecast, the National Association of Realtors said; August figures were revised lower. NAR said August and September remain the weakest months this year, and rising mortgage rates plus elevated home prices have reduced the pool of qualified buyers. Realtor.com estimates recent rate repricing over the past five weeks has trimmed buyer purchasing power by just over 6% — roughly double the typical seasonal discount — a move likely to further suppress housing activity through year-end.

【全國行業大咖齊聚河南許昌，學習“胖東來經驗”】10月13日下午，許昌市委黨校報告廳座無虛席。由商務部主辦的全國零售業創新轉型培訓正式開班，接下來三天，商務部流通發展司、研究院，各省商務主管部門、行業協會、專家學者，以及胖東來、信譽樓、華潤永珍城、長春“這有山”、北京稻香村等企業負責人百餘人同堂參訓。本次培訓不坐而論道：學員將聽取胖東來發展經驗、企業文化與採購管理，調研天使城、中央廚房或物流中心；同時安排信譽樓講百貨自營、永珍城講商圈運營、“這有山”講場景營造、稻香村講老字號煥新。政企協研同堂，目的不是複製一家“網紅店”，而是把“顧客為什麼願意來、員工為什麼願意幹、商品為什麼值得信”拆成供應鏈、組織架構和服務標準。（河南釋出）

### 2026-10-13 22:06:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Existing Home Sales #Importance 2

US Existing Home Sales Remain Weak — Existing home sales in the United States rose by 1.5% from the two-year low in the previous month to a seasonally adjusted annualized rate of 3.99 million units in September of 2026, not too far from market expectations of 3.95 million. Total housing inventory reached 1.6 million units with the increase, 1.9% higher than the previous month to represent 4.8 months of supply under the latest sales rate. Sales recovered slightly in the South (2.8% to 1.86 million) and the West (2.8% to 0.73 million), offsetting a drop in the Northeast (-2.1% to 0.47 million) and a stall in the Midwest (at 0.93 million). “Despite the small monthly gain on a seasonally adjusted basis, August and September marked the two slowest months for home sales this year,” said NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun. “Further increases in mortgage rates in recent weeks mean fewer buyers can qualify to purchase a home.”
